# The Tide Mother — Video Prompts for Grok Imagine (3–6 s clips)

*172 beats, 436 clips, 31:03 total. Generate each clip as a **6-second** Grok video from its own image, then trim it to the length shown. Each clip has at most **one speaking character**, and their voice line is repeated in every clip so the voice stays the same.*

**How to use in Grok Imagine**
- Upload the clip's image (for example Clip 099-2), choose **Make video**, set **16:9** and **6 s**, and paste the prompt. Trim to the length shown.
- Put the clips of a beat back to back in order: -1, -2, -3…
- **Narration is recorded separately** (Grok or TTS) with the narrator voice and laid over the clips in the edit. Grok clips carry only on-screen dialogue and sound.
